<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source reports an investigation and punishment ordered by Umar. Preserve the account as a Companion-era report and do not label it Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Sulayman ibn Yasar from Abu Waqid al-Laythi that a man came to Umar ibn al-Khattab while he was in ash-Sham . He mentioned to him that he had found a man with his wife Umar sent Abu Waqid al-Laythi to the wife to question her about that. He came to her while there were women around her and mentioned to her what her husband had mentioned to Umar ibn al- Khattab, and informed her that she would not be punished on his word and began to suggest to her by that, that she should retract. She refused to retract and held firm to confession. Umar gave the order and she was stoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1508]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1508" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1508</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1508 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू वाक़िद लैथी से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) के पास एक शख़्स आया जब आप शाम में थे। उसने बयान किया के मैंने अपनी औरत के साथ एक मर्द को पाया। आप (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) ने अबू वाक़िद को भेजा के औरत से जा कर पूछे। वो औरत के पास गये, वहाँ और औरतें भी बैठी थी। उन्होंने वो कहा जो उसके ख़ाविंद ने हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) से बयान किया था और ये भी कह दिया के ख़ाविंद के कहने से तुझे मुआख़ज़ा न होगा। वो उसको सिखाने लगे इस क़िस्म की बातें ताकि वो इक़रार न करे, लेकिन उसने न माना और ज़िना का इक़रार किया। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) ने उसको रजम का हुकुम दिया और वो रजम की गयी।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1508" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابو واقد لیثی سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس ایک شخص آیا جب کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم شام میں تھے اس نے بیان کیا کہ میں نے اپنی عورت کے ساتھ ایک مرد کو پایا آپ نے ابوواقد کو بھیجا کہ عورت سے جا کر پوچھے وہ عورت کے پاس گئے اس کے پاس اور عورتیں بیٹھی تھیں انہوں نے کہا وہ جو اس کے خاوند نے حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے بیان کیا تھا اور یہ بھی کہہ دیا کہ خاوند کے کہنے سے تجھے مواخذہ نہ ہوگا اس کو سکھانے بھی لگے اس قسم کی باتیں تاکہ وہ اقرار نہ کرے لیکن اس نے نہ مانا اور اقرار کیا زنا کا حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے اس کو رجم کا حکم کیا اور رجم کی گئی ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1508" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1508]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1508" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1508 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Waqid Laithi se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas ek shakhs aaya jab aap Shaam mein the. Usne bayan kiya ke maine apni aurat ke saath ek mard ko paya. Aap (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Abu Waqid ko bheja ke aurat se ja kar puche. Wo aurat ke paas gaye, wahan aur auratein bhi baithi thi. Unhone wo kaha jo uske khawind ne Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se bayan kiya tha aur ye bhi keh diya ke khawind ke kehne se tujhe muakhaza na hoga. Wo usko sikhane lage is qisam ki baatein taake wo iqrar na kare, lekin usne na mana aur zina ka iqrar kiya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne usko rajam ka hukum diya aur wo rajam ki gayi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1508" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1493

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1509)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. The exact complete source-language field was individually read. The selected span is a locally explicit report or attributed abbreviated report. Independent statements, legal discussion, and explanatory material outside the selected span remain source context. Boundaries apply only to this field; no source wording, canonical identity, or translation equivalence is invented.</p>

<p dir="rtl">فَقَدْ رَجَمَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1509]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1509" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1509</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1509 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَهُ يَقُولُ لَمَّا صَدَرَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ مِنْ مِنًى أَنَاخَ بِالأَبْطَحِ ثُمَّ كَوَّمَ كَوْمَةً بَطْحَاءَ ثُمَّ طَرَحَ عَلَيْهَا رِدَاءَهُ وَاسْتَلْقَى ثُمَّ مَدَّ يَدَيْهِ إِلَى السَّمَاءِ فَقَالَ اللَّهُمَّ كَبِرَتْ سِنِّي وَضَعُفَتْ قُوَّتِي وَانْتَشَرَتْ رَعِيَّتِي ‏.‏ فَاقْبِضْنِي إِلَيْكَ غَيْرَ مُضَيِّعٍ وَلاَ مُفَرِّطٍ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَدِمَ الْمَدِينَةَ فَخَطَبَ النَّاسَ فَقَالَ أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ قَدْ سُنَّتْ لَكُمُ السُّنَنُ وَفُرِضَتْ لَكُمُ الْفَرَائِضُ وَتُرِكْتُمْ عَلَى الْوَاضِحَةِ إِلاَّ أَنْ تَضِلُّوا بِالنَّاسِ يَمِينًا وَشِمَالاً وَضَرَبَ بِإِحْدَى يَدَيْهِ عَلَى الأُخْرَى ثُمَّ قَالَ إِيَّاكُمْ أَنْ تَهْلِكُوا عَنْ آيَةِ الرَّجْمِ أَنْ يَقُولَ قَائِلٌ لاَ نَجِدُ حَدَّيْنِ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1509" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl"> وَرَجَمْنَا وَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لَوْلاَ أَنْ يَقُولَ النَّاسُ زَادَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ تَعَالَى ‏.‏ لَكَتَبْتُهَا الشَّيْخُ وَالشَّيْخَةُ فَارْجُمُوهُمَا الْبَتَّةَ ‏.‏ فَإِنَّا قَدْ قَرَأْنَاهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ قَالَ يَحْيَى بْنُ سَعِيدٍ قَالَ سَعِيدُ بْنُ الْمُسَيَّبِ فَمَا انْسَلَخَ ذُو الْحِجَّةِ حَتَّى قُتِلَ عُمَرُ رَحِمَهُ اللَّهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ قَوْلُهُ الشَّيْخُ وَالشَّيْخَةُ يَعْنِي الثَّيِّبَ وَالثَّيِّبَةَ فَارْجُمُوهُمَا الْبَتَّةَ ‏.‏ حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ عَنِ الَّذِي يَعْمَلُ عَمَلَ قَوْمِ لُوطٍ فَقَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ عَلَيْهِ الرَّجْمُ أَحْصَنَ أَوْ لَمْ يُحْصِنْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1509" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. The selected passage is a locally explicit Prophetic report. Surrounding companion, jurist, compiler and explanatory source material remains under Comments. No source wording is altered and no authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, stoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1509]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1509" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1509</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1509 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that Yahya ibn Said heard Said ibn al- Musayyab say, &quot;When Umar ibn al-Khattab came from Mina, he made his camel kneel at al-Abtah, and then he gathered a pile of small stones and cast his cloak over them and dropped to the ground. Then he raised his hands to the sky and said, &#x27;O Allah! I have become old and my strength has weakened. My flock is scattered. Take me to You with nothing missed out and without having neglected anything.&#x27; Then he went to Madina and addressed the people. He said, &#x27;People! Sunan have been laid down for you. Obligations have been placed upon you. You have been left with a clear way unless you lead people astray right and left.&#x27; He struck one of his hands on the other and then said, &#x27;Take care lest you destroy the ayat of stoning so that one will say, &quot;We do not find two hadds in the Book of Allah.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1509" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">, so we have stoned. By He in Whose Hand my self is, had it not been that people would say that Umar ibn al-Khattab has added to the Book of Allah ta- ala, we would have written it, &quot;The full-grown man and the full-grown woman, stone them absolutely.&quot; We have certainly recited that.&#x27;&quot; Malik said, &quot;Yahya ibn Said said Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &#x27;Dhu&#x27;l-Hijja had not passed before Umar was murdered, may Allah have mercy on him.&#x27; &quot; Yahya said that he had heard Malik say, &quot;As for his words &#x27;The full-grown man and the full-grown woman&#x27; he meant, &#x27;The man and the woman who have been married, stone them absolutely</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1509" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मलिक ने रिवायत की कि याह्या इब्न साइड ने सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब को कहते सुना: जब उमर इब्न अल-खत्ताब (रा) मीणा से आये तो उन्हों ने अपनी ऊँटनी बितायी... और फ़रमाया: &#x27;अगर लोग यह न कहते कि उमर ने किताब-अल्लाह में इज़ाफ़ा कर दिया है तो हम यह आयत लिख देते: बूढ़े मर्द और बूढ़ी औरत</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ، أُتِيَ بِامْرَأَةٍ قَدْ وَلَدَتْ فِي سِتَّةِ أَشْهُرٍ فَأَمَرَ بِهَا أَنْ تُرْجَمَ فَقَالَ عَلِيُّ بْنُ أَبِي طَالِبٍ لَيْسَ ذَلِكَ عَلَيْهَا إِنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى يَقُولُ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَحَمْلُهُ وَفِصَالُهُ ثَلاَثُونَ شَهْرًا‏}‏ وَقَالَ ‏{‏وَالْوَالِدَاتُ يُرْضِعْنَ أَوْلاَدَهُنَّ حَوْلَيْنِ كَامِلَيْنِ لِمَنْ أَرَادَ أَنْ يُتِمَّ الرَّضَاعَةَ‏}‏ فَالْحَمْلُ يَكُونُ سِتَّةَ أَشْهُرٍ فَلاَ رَجْمَ عَلَيْهَا ‏.‏ فَبَعَثَ عُثْمَانُ بْنُ عَفَّانَ فِي أَثَرِهَا فَوَجَدَهَا قَدْ رُجِمَتْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1510" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Ali ibn Abi Talib (companion); Uthman ibn Affan (companion). This field contains two distinct reports: Ali&#x27;s argument to Uthman in a reported case, and a separate question to Ibn Shihab. Keep two report groups. The Quran wording quoted in Ali&#x27;s argument remains within that report; the parenthetical surah locators are retained as source notes. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). This field contains two distinct reports: Ali&#x27;s argument to Uthman in a reported case, and a separate question to Ibn Shihab. Keep two report groups. The Quran wording quoted in Ali&#x27;s argument remains within that report; the parenthetical surah locators are retained as source notes. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he had heard that Uthman ibn Affan was brought a woman who had given birth after six months and he ordered her to be stoned. Ali ibn Abi Talib said to him, &quot;She does not deserve that. Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;Their carrying and weaning is thirty months,&#x27;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> and he said, &#x27;Mothers suckle their children for two full years for whoever wishes to complete the suckling.&#x27;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> Pregnancy can then be six months, so she does not deserve to be stoned.&quot; Uthman ibn Affan sent for her and found that she had already been stoned. </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he asked Ibn Shihab about someone who committed sodomy. Ibn Shihab said, &quot;He is to be stoned, whether or not he is muhsan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1510]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने रिवायत की के उस्मान इब्न अफ्फान (रज़ि) के पास एक ऐसी औरत लाई गई जिस ने छह माह बाद बच्चे को जनम दिया था, उन्हों ने उसे रज़म करने का हुक्म दिया। अली इब्न अबी तालिब (रज़ि) ने फ़रमाया: &#x27;यह इसकी मुस्तहिक नहीं है, अल्लाह फ़रमाता है उनका हमल और दूध छुड़ाना तीस महीने है।&#x27; चुनांचे हमल छह माह का हो सकता है।</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے روایت کی کہ عثمان بن عفان رضی اللہ عنہ کے پاس ایک ایسی عورت لائی گئی جس نے چھ ماہ بعد بچے کو جنم دیا تھا، انہوں نے اسے رجم کرنے کا حکم دیا۔ علی بن ابی طالب رضی اللہ عنہ نے فرمایا: &#x27;یہ اس کی مستحق نہیں ہے، اللہ فرماتا ہے ان کا حمل اور دودھ چھڑانا تیس مہینے ہے۔&#x27; چنانچہ حمل چھ ماہ کا ہو سکتا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne riwayat ki ke Uthman ibn Affan (RA) ke paas ek aisi aurat layi gayi jis ne chah mah baad bachay ko janam diya tha, unhon ne usay rajam karne ka hukm diya. Ali ibn Abi Talib (RA) ne farmaya: &#x27;Yeh iski mustahiq nahi hai, Allah farmata hai unka haml aur doodh chhurana tees mahine hai.&#x27; Chunanche haml chah mah ka ho sakta hai.</p>

## Narration 1495

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1511)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، اعْتَرَفَ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ بِالزِّنَا عَلَى عَهْدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَدَعَا لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِسَوْطٍ فَأُتِيَ بِسَوْطٍ مَكْسُورٍ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ فَوْقَ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَأُتِيَ بِسَوْطٍ جَدِيدٍ لَمْ تُقْطَعْ ثَمَرَتُهُ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ دُونَ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَأُتِيَ بِسَوْطٍ قَدْ رُكِبَ بِهِ وَلاَنَ فَأَمَرَ بِهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَجُلِدَ ثُمَّ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ قَدْ آنَ لَكُمْ أَنْ تَنْتَهُوا عَنْ حُدُودِ اللَّهِ مَنْ أَصَابَ مِنْ هَذِهِ الْقَاذُورَاتِ شَيْئًا فَلْيَسْتَتِرْ بِسِتْرِ اللَّهِ فَإِنَّهُ مَنْ يُبْدِي لَنَا صَفْحَتَهُ نُقِمْ عَلَيْهِ كِتَابَ اللَّهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Zayd ibn Aslam that a man confessed to fornication in the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, called for a whip, and he was brought a broken whip. He said, &quot;Above this,&quot; and he was brought a new whip whose knots had not been cut yet. He said, &quot;Below this,&quot; and he was brought a whip which had been used and made flexible. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave the order and he was flogged. Then he said, &quot;People! The time has come for you to observe the limits of Allah. Whoever has had any of these ugly things befall him should cover them up with the veil of Allah. Whoever reveals to us his wrong action, we perform what is in the Book of Allah against him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलाम से रिवायत है के एक शख्स ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में जीने का इकरार किया. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने कूड़ा मंगवाया (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया इस से नरम लाओ. फिर एक कोड़ा आया जो बिल्कुल टूटा हुआ था अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया इस से सख्त लाओ. फिर एक कोड़ा आया जो सवारी में काम आया था और नरम हो गया था (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उस कोड से मारने का हुकुम दिया. बाद उसके फ़रमाया ऐ लोगों! अब वह वक़्त आ गया है के तुम बाज़ रहो अल्लाह की हदों से. जो शख्स इस क़िस्म का कोई गुनाह करे तोह चाहिए के छुपा रहे अल्लाह के परदे में जो कोई खोल देगा अपने परदे को तोह हम मुवाफ़िक़ किताब-इ-अल्लाह के उस पर हद क़ायम करेंगे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے اقرار کیا زنا کا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے زمانے میں آپ نے کوڑا منگوایا تو نیا کوڑا آیا جس کا سرا بھی نہیں کٹا تھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا اس سے نرم لاؤ پھر ایک کوڑا آیا جو بالکل ٹوٹا ہوا تھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرما اس سے سخت لاؤ پھر ایک کوڑا آیا جو سواری میں کام آیا تھا اور نرم ہو گیا تھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے حکم کیا اس کوڑے سے مارنے کا بعد اس کے فرمایا اے لوگوں اب وہ وقت آگیا ہے کہ تم باز رہو اللہ کی حدوں سے جو شخص اس قسم کا کوئی گناہ کرے تو چاہیے کہ چھپا رہے اللہ کے پردے میں اور جو کوئی کھول دے گا اپنے پردے کو تو ہم موافق کتاب اللہ کے اس پر حد قائم کریں گے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein zina ka iqrar kiya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne koda mangwaya, toh naya koda aaya jiska sira bhi nahi kata tha. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya is se naram lao. Phir ek koda aaya jo bilkul tota hua tha, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya is se sakht lao. Phir ek koda aaya jo sawari mein kaam aaya tha aur naram ho gaya tha, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne us kode se marne ka hukum diya. Baad uske farmaya ae logon! Ab woh waqt aa gaya hai ke tum baaz raho Allah ki hadon se. Jo shakhs is qism ka koi gunah kare toh chahiye ke chupa rahe Allah ke parde mein, aur jo koi khol dega apne parde ko toh hum muwafiq kitab-e-Allah ke us par had qaim karenge.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1496

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1512)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1512]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1512" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1512</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1512 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ صَفِيَّةَ بِنْتَ أَبِي عُبَيْدٍ، أَخْبَرَتْهُ أَنَّ أَبَا بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقَ أُتِيَ بِرَجُلٍ قَدْ وَقَعَ عَلَى جَارِيَةٍ بِكْرٍ فَأَحْبَلَهَا ثُمَّ اعْتَرَفَ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ بِالزِّنَا وَلَمْ يَكُنْ أَحْصَنَ فَأَمَرَ بِهِ أَبُو بَكْرٍ فَجُلِدَ الْحَدَّ ثُمَّ نُفِيَ إِلَى فَدَكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1512" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Bakr as-Siddiq (companion). Select the report of Abu Bakr&#x27;s action through the stated banishment destination. The parenthetical distance from Madina and Malik&#x27;s following legal explanation remain source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi that Safiyya bint Abi Ubayd informed him that a man who had had intercourse with a virgin slave- girl and made her pregnant was brought to Abu Bakr as-Siddiq. He confessed to fornication, and he was not muhsan. Abu Bakr gave the order and he was flogged with the hadd punishment. Then he was banished to Fadak</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1512]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1512" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1512</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1512 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">, (thirty miles from Madina). Malik spoke about a person who confessed to fornication and then retracted it and said, &quot;I didn&#x27;t do it. I said that for such-and-such a reason,&quot; and he mentioned the reason. Malik said, &quot;That is accepted from him and the hadd is not imposed on him. That is because the hadd is what is for Allah, and it is only applied by one of two means, either by a clear proof which establishes guilt or by a confession which is persisted in so that the hadd is imposed. If someone persists in his confession, the hadd is imposed on him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I have not seen the people of knowledge exiling slaves who have committed adultery</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1512" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1512 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सफिया बिंत अबी उबैद से रिवायत है के लोग अबू बक्र (रज़िअल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास एक शख़्स को लाए जिसने एक बकरा लोंदी से ज़िना करके उसको हमिला कर दिया था। बाद उसके ज़िना का इक़रार किया और वह मुह्सन न था। हज़रत अबू बक्र (रज़िअल्लाहु अन्हु) ने हुकुम दिया उसको कोड़ा मारने का, उसको हद पड़ी, बाद उसके निकल दिया गया फ़दक की तरफ जो मदीना से दो दिन की राह पर है। कहा मालिक ने: जो शख़्स ज़िना का इक़रार करे बाद उसके मुंकिर हो जाए और कहे मैं ने ज़िना नहीं किया बल्कि मैं ने फ़लाना काम किया (जैसे अपनी औरत से हालत-ए-हैज़ में जमा किया जिसे उसने ज़िना समझा) तो उस पर हद न पड़ेगी, क्यूंके हद पड़ने में या तो गवाह आदिल होने चाहिए या इक़रार हो जिस पर वह क़ायम रहे हद पड़ने तक।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1512" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">صفیہ بنت ابی عبید سے روایت ہے کہ لوگ ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس ایک شخص کو لائے جس نے ایک باکرہ لونڈی سے زنا کر کے اس کو حاملہ کر دیا تھا بعد اس کے زنا کا اقرار کیا اور وہ محصن نہ تھا حضرت ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے حکم کیا اس کو کوڑا مارنے کا اس کو حد پڑی بعد اس کے نکال دیا گیا فدک کی طرف ایک موضع ہے مدینہ سے دو دن کی راہ پر ۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص زنا کا اقرار کرے بعد اس کے منکر ہوجائے اور کہے میں نے زنا نہیں کیا بلکہ میں نے فلانا کام کیا (جیسے اپنی عورت سے حالت حیض میں جماع کیا اس کو زنا سمجھا) تو اس پر حد نہ پڑے گی کیونکہ حد پڑنے میں یا تو گواہ عادل ہونے چاہئیں یا اقرار ہو جس پر وہ قائم رہے حد پڑنے تک۔</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1512 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Safiyah bint Abi Ubaid se riwayat hai ke log Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas ek shakhs ko laye jisne ek bakra londi se zina karke usko hamila kar diya tha. Baad uske zina ka iqrar kiya aur woh muhsan na tha. Hazrat Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne hukum diya usko koda marne ka, usko had padi, baad uske nikal diya gaya Fadak ki taraf jo Madina se do din ki raah par hai. Kaha Malik ne: Jo shakhs zina ka iqrar kare baad uske munkir ho jaye aur kahe main ne zina nahi kiya balki main ne falana kaam kiya (jaise apni aurat se halat-e-haiz mein jama kiya jise usne zina samjha) toh us par had na padegi, kyunke had padne mein ya toh gawah aadil hone chahiye ya iqrar ho jis par woh qaim rahe had padne tak.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1512" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1497

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1513)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، وَزَيْدِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم سُئِلَ عَنِ الأَمَةِ إِذَا زَنَتْ وَلَمْ تُحْصِنْ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنْ زَنَتْ فَاجْلِدُوهَا ثُمَّ إِنْ زَنَتْ فَاجْلِدُوهَا ثُمَّ إِنْ زَنَتْ فَاجْلِدُوهَا ثُمَّ بِيعُوهَا وَلَوْ بِضَفِيرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ لاَ أَدْرِي أَبَعْدَ الثَّالِثَةِ أَوِ الرَّابِعَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Ubaydullah ibn Abdullah ibn Utba ibn Masud from Abu Hurayra and Zayd ibn Khalid al-Juhani that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was asked about a slave-girl who committed fornication and was not muhsana. He said, &quot;If she commits fornication, then flog her. If she commits fornication again, then flog her, and if she commits fornication again, then sell her, if only for a rope.&quot; Ibn Shihab added, &quot;I don&#x27;t know whether it was three or four times</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) और ज़ैद बिन खालिद जुहनी से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से किसी ने पूछा के लोंडी ग़ैर-मुहसना जब ज़िना करे तोह क्या हुकुम है? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के अगर वह ज़िना करे तोह उसको कोड़े मार कोड़े मारो. बाद उसके चौथी मर्तबा या तीसरी मरतबा के बाद आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया बेच डालो ऐसी लोंडी को अगरचे एक रस्सी के एवज में ही हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ اور زید بن خالد جہنی سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم سے کسی نے پوچھا کہ لونڈی غیر محصنہ جب زنا کرے تو کیا حکم ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ اگر وہ زنا کرے تو اس کو کوڑے مار پھر اگر زنا کرے تو پھر اس کو کوڑے مارو پھر اگر زنا کرے تو پھر اس کو کوڑے مارو بعد اس کے چوتھی مرتبہ یا تیسری مرتبہ کے بعد آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا بیچ ڈالو ایسی لونڈی کو اگرچہ ایک رسی کے عوض میں ہو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) aur Zaid bin Khalid Juhani se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se kisi ne pucha ke londi ghair-muhsana jab zina kare toh kya hukum hai? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke agar woh zina kare toh usko kode maar, phir agar zina kare toh phir usko kode maro, phir agar zina kare toh phir usko kode maro. Baad uske chauthi martaba ya teesri martaba ke baad Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya bech daalo aisi londi ko agarche ek rassi ke ewaz mein hi ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1498

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدًا، كَانَ يَقُومُ عَلَى رَقِيقِ الْخُمُسِ وَأَنَّهُ اسْتَكْرَهَ جَارِيَةً مِنْ ذَلِكَ الرَّقِيقِ فَوَقَعَ بِهَا فَجَلَدَهُ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَنَفَاهُ وَلَمْ يَجْلِدِ الْوَلِيدَةَ لأَنَّهُ اسْتَكْرَهَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1514" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Nafi&#x27;s report describes Umar&#x27;s treatment of the coercing slave and the coerced slave-girl. Keep the source account and its distinction intact, without Prophetic attribution.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi that a slave was in charge of the slaves in the khumus and he forced a slave-girl among those slaves against her will and had intercourse with her. Umar ibn al-Khattab had him flogged and banished him, and he did not flog the slave-girl because the slave had forced her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1514]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1514" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नफ़ी से रिवायत है के एक घुलम मुक़र्रर था उन ग़ुलामों और लौंडियों पर जो ख़ुम्स में आई थीं. उसने उन में से एक लोंदी से ज़बरदस्ती जमा किया. हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने उसको कोड़े मारे और निकल दिया न मारा क्यूंकि उस पर जब्र हुआ था.</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ke ek ghulam muqarrar tha un ghulamon aur londiyon par jo khums mein aayi thin. Usne un mein se ek londi se zabardasti jama kiya. Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne usko kode mare aur nikal diya, aur londi ko na mara kyunke us par jabr hua tha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1514" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَيَّاشِ بْنِ أَبِي رَبِيعَةَ الْمَخْزُومِيَّ قَالَ أَمَرَنِي عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ فِي فِتْيَةٍ مِنْ قُرَيْشٍ فَجَلَدْنَا وَلاَئِدَ مِنْ وَلاَئِدِ الإِمَارَةِ خَمْسِينَ خَمْسِينَ فِي الزِّنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1515" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source reports orders attributed to Umar and the narrator&#x27;s execution of them. Retain the source&#x27;s own historical wording and narrator framing.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said that Sulayman ibn Yasar informed him that Abdullah ibn Abbas ibn Abi Rabia al-Makhzumi said, &quot;Umar ibn al-Khattab gave me orders about the slaves of Quraysh and we flogged some of the slave-girls of the muslim lands fifty times each for fornication</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1515]</p>

## Narration 1500

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1516)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1516]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1516" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1516</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1516 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَلَدَ عُمَرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ عَبْدًا فِي فِرْيَةٍ ثَمَانِينَ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو الزِّنَادِ فَسَأَلْتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَامِرِ بْنِ رَبِيعَةَ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ أَدْرَكْتُ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ وَعُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ وَالْخُلَفَاءَ هَلُمَّ جَرًّا فَمَا رَأَيْتُ أَحَدًا جَلَدَ عَبْدًا فِي فِرْيَةٍ أَكْثَرَ مِنْ أَرْبَعِينَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1516" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Abu&#x27;z-Zinad (successor); Abdullah ibn Amir ibn Rabia (unresolved). Select Abu&#x27;z-Zinad&#x27;s reported case and subsequent question contrasting earlier caliphal practice. Malik&#x27;s preceding legal discussion is not part of that narrated report.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad that he said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz flogged a slave with eighty lashes for slander.&quot; Abu&#x27;z-Zinad said, &quot;I asked Abdullah ibn Amir ibn Rabia about that. He said, &#x27;I saw Umar ibn al-Khattab, Uthman ibn Affan, the Khalifs, and so on, and I did not see any of them flog a slave more than forty lashes for slander</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1516]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1516" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1516</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1516 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;The position with us about a woman who is found to be pregnant and has no husband and she says, &#x27;I was forced,&#x27; or she says, &#x27;I was married,&#x27; is that it is not accepted from her and the hadd is inflicted on her unless she has a clear proof of what she claims about the marriage or that she was forced or she comes bleeding if she was a virgin or she calls out for help so that someone comes to her and she is in that state or what resembles it of the situation in which the violation occurred.&quot; He said, &quot;If she does not produce any of those, the hadd is inflicted on her and what she claims of that is not accepted from her.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A raped woman cannot marry until she has restored herself by three menstrual periods.&quot; He said, &quot;If she doubts her periods, she does not marry until she has freed herself of that doubt.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1516" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Zinad se riwayat hai ke Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne ek ghulam ko had-e-qazf ke assi (80) kode lagaye, toh main ne Abdullah bin Aamir se pucha. Unhone kaha main ne Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu), Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) aur khulafa ko unke baad dekha ke kisi ne ghulam ko had-e-qazf mein chalis kode se zyada nahi lagaye.</p>
